# Docs Archive C1c Remaining Docs Classification Dry Run

Status: C1c remaining-docs classification completed validated
Mode: A4.8 local documentation and reference audit only
Prior map: docs/archive/DOCS_ARCHIVE_C1B_REFERENCE_MAP_DRY_RUN.md
Base policy: docs/archive/DOCS_ARCHIVE_REFERENCE_POLICY.md

This report classifies the remaining top-level historical docs after the C1a exact-file move. It does not authorize additional movement, reference rewriting, wrappers, deletion, staging, committing, or pushing.

## Boundary

This C1c dry run did not:

- move docs
- delete files
- create wrappers
- rewrite references
- change validator behavior
- split scripts
- process runs/
- stage, commit, push, tag, release, or deploy
- execute A5
- call provider, plugin, API, DailyNote, VCP memory, or runtime
- read .env, secrets, private paths, real manifests, VCPChat, or VCPToolBox

## Classification Rules

| Class | Rule | Future handling |
| --- | --- | --- |
| validator-blocked | referenced by scripts/ or tests/ | keep original path until validator/test compatibility exists |
| wrapper-required | referenced by README.md, PROJECT_MASTER_PLAN.md, AGENTS.md, .agent_board/, or another operational surface, without scripts/tests refs | move only with reference update or human-navigation wrapper plan |
| docs-only-reference | referenced only by docs/ outside docs/archive/ | move only with docs link updates or wrappers in the same authorized batch |
| future exact-move candidates | zero external references in the scanned scope | eligible for a future exact-file move package, not moved by this dry run |
